HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0d037c21651d383d8346","text":"1. **Creation as measured leveling before guidance.** Lexical evidence: خ ل ق B001/B003 (measuring and complete form), س و ي B002/B006 (straight completion and even middle), ق د ر B001/B005 in 87:3. Structural cue: خلق فسوى is immediately followed by قدر فهدى. Assigned role: \"created\" supplies measured form; \"proportioned\" makes that form internally straight enough to receive direction. Abductive move: guidance requires prior fitted structure. Reading change: the ayah reads less as two generic divine acts and more as the first stage in an engineering sequence: measure, straighten, assign, guide.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0e81844734d0767641bb","text":"2. **Formation includes an unseen interior.** `خ ل ق/B004` gives inner disposition and character, while `خ ل ق/B003` and `س و ي/B002` cover visible, well-proportioned form. The later contrast in 87:7 between `ٱلْجَهْرَ` and `مَا يَخْفَىٰ` supplies the structural inside/outside pair. The hidden-disposition branch assigns creation an inward object alongside outward shape. I infer that the maker’s knowledge of both domains permits both to belong to the forming act. The ayah consequently reads as integration of exterior form and interior capacity, not bodily shaping alone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B008","root_000766/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1b2d4ecb94172a682624","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:2:2\",\"root_000434\",[\"B008\"]],[\"87:2:3\",\"root_000766\",[\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Nesnesi belirtilmeyen yaratma, 87:4'te otlağın çıkacağı düzgün ve üretken zeminin hazırlanmasını da kapsayabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_266c957675f0bb07cd16","text":"87:14 made the moral branch more visible: تزكى looks like a later human answer to the earlier divine تسوية. 87:4-5 also surprised the ayah by showing that formed greenery can become refuse, so \"made even\" does not mean static permanence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_28e241edec0f81b49751","text":"4. **Character formation under lordly nurture.** Lexical evidence: خلق B004 inner disposition/character; ربب B002 nurture/completion in 87:1; زكو B002 purity/rectitude in 87:14. Structural cue: the surah later shifts from cosmic formation to moral purification. Assigned role: خلق's character branch bridges created form and purified conduct. Abductive move: the packet gives character, nurture, and purification; I infer that \"made even\" also points toward rectified moral capacity. Reading change: the ayah becomes anthropological as well as cosmological: formed beings are fitted for ethical purification.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2a6f0101fb1f0d49dd73","text":"1. **Creation is measured before it is completed.** `خ ل ق/B001` supplies measuring and proportioning before action, and `س و ي/B002` supplies straightening and sound completion. The near repetition in 87:3, `قَدَّرَ فَهَدَىٰ`, separates a two-stage mechanism: prior specification, then completed form, followed by assigned measure and course. `خَلَقَ` sets the plan and `سَوَّىٰ` realizes a balanced result. I infer this division of labor from the parallel `فـ` sequences. The ayah reads less as two synonyms and more as design brought to finished integrity.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3246b3c0f2128fac6d32","text":"3. **Hidden smooth-ground and pasture activation.** Lexical evidence: خلق B008 smooth/level surface; سوي B009 wide smooth open ground; مرعى in 87:4 with رعي B001 grazing/pasture. Structural cue: \"made level/sound\" precedes the bringing out of pasture. Assigned role: the forming verb also prepares an ecological surface. Abductive move: the packet supplies level-surface branches and pasture; I infer that land-readiness is one concrete arena of the creating/settling action. Reading change: \"He created and made even\" can include the terrain of life, not only bodies.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B004","root_000766/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_32887c15a363d7788b26","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:2:2\",\"root_000434\",[\"B004\"]],[\"87:2:3\",\"root_000766\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Biçim verme, dış görünüşle birlikte daha sonra anma ve arınmaya cevap verebilecek iç yatkınlığı da kapsar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_56354b1c84b143584f1a","text":"1. **Creation as measured formation followed by internal straightening.** Lexical evidence: خلق B001 measuring/proportioning and B002 originating; سوي B002 internal straightness and sound completion. Structural cue: the فـ sequence makes \"created\" move directly into \"made even/sound.\" Assigned role: خلق initiates the entity; سوي completes and straightens it. Abductive move: the packet supplies two verbs and formation branches; I infer a two-stage mechanism from origin to proportion. Reading change: creation is not bare production; it includes aftercare of shape and inner order.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000766/B002","root_000766/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7ba876be3251a46334a0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:2:3\",\"root_000766\",[\"B002\",\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Düzene koyma, 87:3'teki yol göstermeye elverişli bir yön ve denge kazandırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_92721fc3cf57fd495805","text":"3. **Smoothed surface awaiting inscription.** Lexical evidence: خ ل ق B008 (smooth level surface), س و ي B009 (wide/smooth open ground), ق ر ء B001/B002 and ص ح ف B001/B002 later. Structural cue: bodily/earthly forming precedes recitation and sheets. Assigned role: smoothing becomes preparation for legibility, like leveling a surface before marks can hold. Abductive move: physical surface images can analogize to memory and scripture as surfaces that retain order. Reading change: the ayah becomes a pre-literacy image of making a form readable.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B001","root_000766/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9ae6e28b3b26243a37c4","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:2:2\",\"root_000434\",[\"B001\"]],[\"87:2:3\",\"root_000766\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yaratma, önce ölçülmüş bir tasarımın kurulması, sonra varlığın sağlam iç düzenine kavuşturulması olarak iki aşamalı görünür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c4bf90bba84baee5d9e1","text":"2. **Formation as preparation for guidance.** Lexical evidence: سوي B008 aiming toward a person/direction; قدر B005 arranging by estimate and هدي B001 guidance in 87:3. Structural cue: 87:2 and 87:3 are parallel relative clauses, each with a فـ-result. Assigned role: \"settling/straightening\" equips the creature to receive a measured path. Abductive move: the packet gives consecutive clauses; I infer that physical/structural completion enables directional guidance. Reading change: the ayah reads as making beings guidable, not merely symmetrical.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_cacca541553b938bcf4e","text":"3. **The clause can also prepare an ecological surface.** `خ ل ق/B008` supplies a smooth, level surface, and `س و ي/B009` a broad or smooth tract of ground. Immediate emergence of `ٱلْمَرْعَىٰ` in 87:4 makes terrain the material cue. These branches give the two verbs the role of preparing a stable substrate from which pasture can appear. I infer the enabling arrow from the sequence; the ayah leaves the created object unstated. The reading expands from forming organisms to making an inhabitable, productive field.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_eaa16e195002bb397323","text":"2. **Moral/temperamental formation.** Lexical evidence: خ ل ق B004 (inner disposition and character), س و ي B002 (sound character), ز ك و B002 in 87:14 (purity/rectitude), ذ ك ر B003/B009 in 87:9-10. Structural cue: the created/proportioned subject is later divided by remembrance, fear, avoidance, and purification. Assigned role: created form includes the creature's moral aptness, not only body. Abductive move: later responsiveness to reminder presupposes an inwardly configured receptacle. Reading change: \"proportioned\" activates psychological and ethical straightening.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f7448bfa3c8ebd50de20","text":"87:4 made the smooth-ground branches of both roots unexpectedly concrete by placing pasture immediately after the unsupplied object of creation.
